    actualizado_en timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    publicado_en timestamp with time zone,
    consulta_id uuid NOT NULL,
    preguntas_omitidas jsonb NOT NULL
);

    inicio timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    fin timestamp with time zone,
    estado character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    origen character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    especialidad character varying(120) NOT NULL,
    sede character varying(160) NOT NULL,
    consultorio character varying(80) NOT NULL,
    motivo character varying(240) NOT NULL,
    observaciones text NOT NULL,
    confirmada_en timestamp with time zone,
    creado_en timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    actualizado_en timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    confirmada_por_id uuid,
    creada_por_id uuid,
    medico_id uuid,
    paciente_id uuid NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT cita_fin_posterior_inicio CHECK (((fin IS NULL) OR (fin > inicio)))
);

-- Name: clinica_dia_programacionquimioterapia; Type: TABLE; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE public.clinica_dia_programacionquimioterapia (
    id uuid NOT NULL,
    creado_en timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    actualizado_en timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    fecha date NOT NULL,
    turno character varying(2) NOT NULL,
    hora_inicio time without time zone NOT NULL,
    hora_fin time without time zone NOT NULL,
    cama smallint NOT NULL,
    estado character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    origen character varying(12) NOT NULL,
    observaciones text NOT NULL,
    confirmada_en timestamp with time zone,
    cancelada_en timestamp with time zone,
    motivo_cancelacion character varying(255) CONSTRAINT clinica_dia_programacionquimioterap_motivo_cancelacion_not_null NOT NULL,
    recordatorio_estado character varying(16) CONSTRAINT clinica_dia_programacionquimiotera_recordatorio_estado_not_null NOT NULL,
    recordatorio_en timestamp with time zone,
    recordatorio_observacion character varying(255) CONSTRAINT clinica_dia_programacionquimi_recordatorio_observacion_not_null NOT NULL,
    cancelada_por_id uuid,
    cita_id uuid,
    confirmada_por_id uuid,
    programada_por_id uuid,
    recordatorio_por_id uuid,
    solicitud_id uuid NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT clinica_dia_programacionquimioterapia_cama_check CHECK ((cama >= 0)),
    CONSTRAINT quim_prog_cama_rango CHECK (((cama >= 1) AND (cama <= 8))),
    CONSTRAINT quim_prog_horas_validas CHECK ((hora_fin > hora_inicio))
);

CREATE TABLE public.clinica_dia_solicitudquimioterapia (

    actualizado_en timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,

    nombre_completo_importado character varying(241) CONSTRAINT clinica_dia_solicitudquimiot_nombre_completo_importado_not_null NOT NULL,
    historia_clinica_importada character varying(32) CONSTRAINT clinica_dia_solicitudquimio_historia_clinica_importada_not_null NOT NULL,
    fecha_nacimiento_importada date,
    telefono character varying(20) NOT NULL,
    procedencia character varying(160) NOT NULL,
    diagnostico character varying(240) NOT NULL,
    protocolo character varying(240) NOT NULL,
    prioridad character varying(8) NOT NULL,
    fecha_preferida date,
    hora_preferida time without time zone,
    duracion_minutos smallint NOT NULL,
    estado character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    origen character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    codigo_externo character varying(80),
    lote_importacion uuid,
    fila_importacion integer,
    huella_importacion character varying(64),
    observaciones text NOT NULL,
    creada_por_id uuid,
    paciente_id uuid,
    CONSTRAINT clinica_dia_solicitudquimioterapia_duracion_minutos_check CHECK ((duracion_minutos >= 0)),
    CONSTRAINT clinica_dia_solicitudquimioterapia_fila_importacion_check CHECK ((fila_importacion >= 0)),
    CONSTRAINT quim_sol_duracion_rango CHECK (((duracion_minutos >= 15) AND (duracion_minutos <= 210)))
);

    id uuid NOT NULL,
    tipo character varying(24) NOT NULL,
    titulo character varying(180) NOT NULL,
    descripcion text NOT NULL,
    archivo character varying(500) NOT NULL,
    nombre_original character varying(255) NOT NULL,
    tipo_mime character varying(120) NOT NULL,
    tamano_bytes bigint NOT NULL,
    sha256 character varying(64) NOT NULL,
    fecha_documento date,
    origen character varying(12) NOT NULL,
    estado character varying(16) NOT NULL,
    creado_en timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    actualizado_en timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    consulta_id uuid,
    paciente_id uuid NOT NULL,
    subido_por_id uuid NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT documentos_documentopaciente_tamano_bytes_check CHECK ((tamano_bytes >= 0))
);
